- **contract-architect**: KeepCon 공유 계약(데이터 모델, Repository/Service 인터페이스, 함수 시그니처, 라우트 이름)의 단일 진실 원천(SSOT)을 정의·유지하는 전문가. 페이지 간 의존성의 기준이 되는 계약을 lib/shared/에 확정한다. 새 모델/함수/라우트가 필요하거나, 페이지 개발자들이 공유 타입에 대해 질문할 때 호출. - **contract-auditor**: Use this agent to find drift between machine-checkable contracts — type hints, function signatures, docstring parameter/return descriptions, and API/OpenAPI/GraphQL schemas — and their actual call-site or handler usage. Operates in Find (extract contracts and compare) or Verify (independently re-check one candidate mismatch) mode. Never extracts or verifies prose documentation, and never fixes contracts or code — it only reports mismatches with both the declared and actual-usage locations. Detecta el stack, la taxonomia de capas, el eje organizativo actual, la convencion destino, la estrategia de imports (relativa o alias) y el comando de build gate. Read-only: nunca modifica archivos. Produce el contrato de migracion que gobierna todas las fases siguientes. - **contract-critic**: Use this agent during the Data-First Engineering Protocol AFTER the data-architect drafts a concept contract and BEFORE the user sees it for approval. - **conventions**: - A parameter noun before a measurement (`при температуре 660 °C`, `со скоростью 60 км/ч`, `диаметром 5 м`) is named by that measurement, so the measurement is an apposition and stays nominative in any case the parameter itself carries. The genitive reading needs an explicit `в` in the source: `при температуре в 660 градусов`. The closed list of these nouns lives in `MEASURE_PARAMETER_HEADS`. - A ([NickZaitsev/ru-normalizr](https://github.com/NickZaitsev/ru-normalizr/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/conventions.md) · ★30)

- **conventions**: - 中文为主，代码/术语保留英文。 - 子文档**主题语义命名**（`architecture.md`、`data-model.md`），**不用数字序号前缀**。 - 标题用 Markdown 天然层级语义式命名，不加 `## 1. X` 这类数字前缀。 - `docs/` 是给人看的解释性文档，`skills/*/SKILL.md` 是给模型看的操作指令，两者分工不重叠。 ([morehao/agent-skills](https://github.com/morehao/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/conventions.md) · ★1)
